   211	
   212	def sanitize_error_text(text: Any, *secrets: str) -> str:
   213	    """Return a credential-free error excerpt safe for committed artifacts."""
   214	
   215	    message = str(text or "").strip()
   216	    for secret in secrets:
   217	        cleaned = str(secret or "").strip()
   218	        if cleaned:
   219	            message = message.replace(cleaned, "[REDACTED]")
   220	    message = _CREDENTIAL_KEY_RE.sub(
   221	        lambda match: f"{match.group(1)}{match.group(2)}[REDACTED]", message
   222	    )
   223	    message = _OPAQUE_BLOB_RE.sub("[REDACTED]", message)
   224	    return message[:300]
   225	
   226	
   227	def contains_credential_header_hint(text: Any) -> list[str]:
   228	    """Return credential header names appearing in plain or serialized form.
   229	
   230	    001A-R1A fix (final review P1#2): serialized header dicts such as
   231	    ``{"Cookie": "sid"}`` or ``{'Authorization': 'Bearer x'}`` put a quote
   232	    between the header name and the separator, so naive ``"cookie:"``
   233	    substring scans miss them while ``sanitize_error_text`` leaves the
   234	    short value intact.  The optional quote/backslash regex (backslashes
   235	    included so JSON-escaped artifacts like ``{\\"Cookie\\": ...}`` stay
   236	    detectable) closes that fail-closed detection gap; empty result means
   237	    no credential header hint found.
   238	    """
   239	
   240	    return sorted(
   241	        {
   242	            match.group(1).lower()
   243	            for match in _CREDENTIAL_HEADER_HINT_RE.finditer(str(text or ""))
   244	        }
   245	    )
   246	
   247	
   248	def classify_error_message(message: str) -> str:
   249	    text = str(message or "")
   250	    lowered = text.lower()
   250	    lowered = text.lower()
   251	    if any(marker in text for marker in _RATE_LIMIT_MARKERS) or any(
   252	        marker in lowered for marker in _RATE_LIMIT_MARKERS_EN
   253	    ):
   254	        return STATE_RATE_LIMITED
   255	    if any(marker in text for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS) or any(
   256	        marker in lowered for marker in _PERMISSION_MARKERS_EN
   257	    ):
   258	        return STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED
   259	    return STATE_QUERY_FAILED
   260	
   261	
   262	def is_transient_network_error(message: str) -> bool:
   263	    lowered = str(message or "").lower()
   264	    return any(marker in lowered for marker in _TRANSIENT_MARKERS)
   265	
   266	
   267	def classify_exception(exc: BaseException) -> str:
   268	    return classify_error_message(str(exc))
   269	
   270	
   271	def canonical_frame_payload(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> str:
   272	    if frame is None or len(frame) == 0:
   273	        return "[]"
   274	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   275	    for row in frame.to_dict(orient="records"):
   276	        clean: dict[str, Any] = {}
   277	        for key, value in row.items():
   278	            try:
   279	                missing = bool(pd.isna(value))
   280	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
   281	                missing = False
   282	            if missing or value is None:
   283	                clean[str(key)] = None
   284	            elif isinstance(value, float) and (math.isnan(value) or math.isinf(value)):
   285	                clean[str(key)] = None
   286	            elif isinstance(value, (str, int, bool)):
   287	                clean[str(key)] = value
   288	            else:
   289	                clean[str(key)] = str(value)
   290	        records.append(clean)
   291	    return json.dumps(records, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
   292	
   293	
   294	def payload_sha256(payload: str) -> str:
   295	    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   296	
   297	
   298	def extract_time_range(frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
   299	    if frame is None or frame.empty:
   300	        return None
   301	    for column in _DATE_RANGE_FIELD_PRIORITY:
   302	        if column not in frame.columns:
   303	            continue
   304	        values = frame[column].astype(str).str.replace(r"\D", "", regex=True)
   305	        values = values[values.str.len().eq(8)]
   306	        if not values.empty:
   307	            return {"field": column, "min": values.min(), "max": values.max()}
   308	    return None
   309	
   310	
   311	def build_params(spec: EndpointSpec, probe_context: Mapping[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
   312	    ts_code = probe_context["ts_code"]
   313	    params: dict[str, str] = {}
   314	    for kind in spec.param_kinds:
   315	        if kind == PARAM_KIND_TS_CODE:
   316	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   317	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_STATEMENT_WINDOW:
   318	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["statement_start"]
   319	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   320	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_RECENT_WINDOW:
   321	            params["start_date"] = probe_context["window_start"]
   322	            params["end_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   323	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE:
   324	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   325	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_PROBE_DATE_WITH_SYMBOL:
   326	            params["ts_code"] = ts_code
   327	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   328	        elif kind == PARAM_KIND_EXCHANGE_PROBE_DATE:
   329	            params["trade_date"] = probe_context["probe_date"]
   330	            params["exchange"] = probe_context["exchange"]
   331	        else:
   332	            raise ValueError(f"unknown param kind: {kind}")
   333	    return params
   334	
   335	
   336	def with_fallback_date(params: Mapping[str, str], fallback_date: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   337	    updated = dict(params)
   338	    updated["trade_date"] = fallback_date
   339	    return updated
   340	
   341	
   342	def permission_status_for(state: str) -> str:
   343	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   344	        return PERMISSION_DENIED
   345	    if state in (STATE_HAS_DATA, STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA, STATE_RATE_LIMITED):
   346	        return PERMISSION_ALLOWED
   347	    return PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   348	
   349	
   350	def _empty_vs_error(state: str) -> str:
   351	    if state == STATE_HAS_DATA:
   352	        return "data_returned"
   353	    if state == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   354	        return "empty_frame_without_error"
   355	    if state == STATE_QUERY_FAILED:
   356	        return "generic_error_raised"
   357	    if state == STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED:
   358	        return "permission_error_raised"
   359	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   360	        return "rate_limit_error_raised"
   361	    return "not_queried"
   362	
   363	
   364	def _base_record(
   365	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   366	    params: Mapping[str, str],
   367	    *,
   368	    tested_at: str,
   369	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   370	    return {
   371	        "endpoint": spec.endpoint,
   372	        "category": spec.category,
   373	        "scope": spec.scope,
   374	        "note": spec.note or None,
   375	        "params": dict(params),
   376	        "tested_at": tested_at,
   377	        "attempts": 1,
   378	        "retry_reason": None,
   379	        "first_attempt_state": None,
   380	        "state": None,
   381	        "permission_status": None,
   382	        "extra_paid": False,
   383	        "row_count": None,
   384	        "column_count": None,
   385	        "columns": None,
   386	        "time_range": None,
   387	        "response_sha256": None,
   388	        "response_sha256_scope": None,
   389	        "empty_vs_error": None,
   390	        "rate_limit": {
   391	            "per_minute": None,
   392	            "per_hour": None,
   393	            "verified_in_run": False,
   394	            "note": "单次探测无法验证限频；仅记录探测时是否触发限流错误",
   395	        },
   396	        "latency_ms": None,
   397	        "error": None,
   398	    }
   399	
   400	
   401	def _apply_frame_result(record: dict[str, Any], frame: pd.DataFrame | None) -> None:
   402	    normalized = frame if isinstance(frame, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()
   403	    record["row_count"] = int(len(normalized))
   404	    record["column_count"] = int(normalized.shape[1]) if not normalized.empty else 0
   405	    record["columns"] = [str(column) for column in normalized.columns] if not normalized.empty else []
   406	    record["time_range"] = extract_time_range(normalized)
   407	    payload = canonical_frame_payload(normalized)
   408	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(payload)
   409	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "frame"
   410	    record["state"] = STATE_HAS_DATA if len(normalized) else STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA
   411	
   412	
   413	def _apply_error_result(
   414	    record: dict[str, Any],
   415	    exc: BaseException,
   416	    *,
   417	    token: str,
   418	) -> None:
   419	    state = classify_exception(exc)
   420	    record["state"] = state
   421	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(exc, token)
   422	    record["response_sha256"] = payload_sha256(sanitize_error_text(exc, token))
   423	    record["response_sha256_scope"] = "sanitized_error"
   424	    record["row_count"] = None
   425	    record["column_count"] = None
   426	    record["columns"] = None
   427	    record["time_range"] = None
   428	    if state == STATE_RATE_LIMITED:
   429	        record["rate_limit"]["verified_in_run"] = True
   430	
   431	
   432	def probe_endpoint(
   433	    query_fn: Callable[..., pd.DataFrame],
   434	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   435	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   436	    *,
   437	    token: str = "",
   438	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   439	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   440	    """Execute at most one probe (plus at most one recorded retry).
   441	
   442	    Retry policy: (1) date-bound endpoints that return an empty frame on the
   443	    probe date may retry once on the fallback trading date; (2) a transient
   444	    network error may retry once with identical params.  Permission denial
   445	    and rate limiting must never be retried.
   446	    """
   447	
   448	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   449	    params = build_params(spec, probe_context)
   450	    record = _base_record(spec, params, tested_at=timestamp)
   451	    attempt_params = params
   452	    started = datetime.now()
   453	    try:
   454	        frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **attempt_params)
   455	    except Exception as exc:
   456	        _apply_error_result(record, exc, token=token)
   457	        record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   458	        if record["state"] == STATE_QUERY_FAILED and is_transient_network_error(str(exc)):
   459	            retry_params = attempt_params
   460	            retry_reason = "transient_network_error_single_retry"
   461	            record["attempts"] = 2
   462	            record["retry_reason"] = retry_reason
   463	            try:
   464	                frame = query_fn(spec.endpoint, **retry_params)
   465	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   466	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   467	            else:
   468	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   469	        record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   470	        record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   471	        record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   472	        return record
   473	    _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   474	    record["first_attempt_state"] = record["state"]
   475	    if spec.date_bound and record["state"] == STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA:
   476	        fallback = probe_context.get("fallback_date", "")
   477	        if fallback:
   478	            record["attempts"] = 2
   479	            record["retry_reason"] = "probe_date_no_rows_retry_on_fallback_trading_date"
   480	            try:
   481	                frame = query_fn(
   482	                    spec.endpoint,
   483	                    **with_fallback_date(attempt_params, fallback),
   484	                )
   485	            except Exception as retry_exc:
   486	                _apply_error_result(record, retry_exc, token=token)
   487	            else:
   488	                _apply_frame_result(record, frame)
   489	    record["latency_ms"] = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
   490	    record["permission_status"] = permission_status_for(record["state"])
   491	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(record["state"])
   492	    return record
   493	
   494	
   495	def not_queried_record(
   496	    spec: EndpointSpec,
   497	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   498	    reason: str,
   499	    *,
   500	    now: Callable[[], datetime] | None = None,
   501	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   502	    timestamp = (now or datetime.now)().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   503	    record = _base_record(spec, build_params(spec, probe_context), tested_at=timestamp)
   504	    record["state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   505	    record["first_attempt_state"] = STATE_NOT_QUERIED
   506	    record["permission_status"] = PERMISSION_UNKNOWN
   507	    record["empty_vs_error"] = _empty_vs_error(STATE_NOT_QUERIED)
   508	    record["error"] = sanitize_error_text(reason)
   509	    return record
   510	
   511	
   512	def build_matrix(
   513	    probe_context: Mapping[str, str],
   514	    records: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   515	    *,
   516	    token_status: str,
   517	    token_source: str = "",
   518	    token_env_override_applied: bool | None = None,
   519	    generated_at: str | None = None,
   520	    excluded: Sequence[Mapping[str, str]] = EXCLUDED_ENDPOINTS,
   521	    state_evidence: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]] | None = None,
   522	    notes: Sequence[str] = (),
   523	) -> dict[str, Any]:
   524	    by_state: dict[str, int] = {}
   525	    by_permission: dict[str, int] = {}
   526	    for record in records:
   527	        by_state[record["state"]] = by_state.get(record["state"], 0) + 1
   528	        by_permission[record["permission_status"]] = (
   529	            by_permission.get(record["permission_status"], 0) + 1
   530	        )
   531	    evidence: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
   532	        state: {
   533	            "fixture": None,
   534	            "observed_in_real_run": False,
   535	            "real_run_examples": [],
   536	        }
   537	        for state in (
   538	            STATE_HAS_DATA,
   539	            STATE_NORMAL_NO_DATA,
   540	            STATE_QUERY_FAILED,
   541	            STATE_PERMISSION_DENIED,
   542	            STATE_RATE_LIMITED,
   543	            STATE_NOT_QUERIED,
   544	        )
   545	    }
   546	    for state, info in (state_evidence or {}).items():
   547	        if state in evidence:
   548	            evidence[state].update(info)
   549	    for record in records:
   550	        state = record["state"]
   551	        if state in evidence and not evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"]:
   552	            evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"] = True
   553	        if state in evidence and record["endpoint"] not in evidence[state]["real_run_examples"]:
   554	            evidence[state]["real_run_examples"].append(record["endpoint"])
   555	    for state in evidence:
   556	        if not evidence[state]["observed_in_real_run"]:
   557	            evidence[state]["real_run_examples"] = []
   558	    return {
   559	        "schema_version": MATRIX_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   560	        "task_id": TASK_ID,
   561	        "generated_at": generated_at
   562	        or datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
   563	        "token_status": token_status,
   564	        "token_source": token_source or None,
   565	        "token_env_override_applied": token_env_override_applied,
   566	        "probe": {
   567	            "symbol": probe_context["ts_code"],
   568	            "exchange": probe_context.get("exchange"),
   569	            "probe_date": probe_context["probe_date"],
   570	            "fallback_date": probe_context.get("fallback_date"),
   571	            "window_start": probe_context.get("window_start"),
   572	            "statement_start": probe_context.get("statement_start"),
   573	            "policy": (
   574	                "每个 endpoint 最多一次正常探测；仅瞬态网络错误或日期型空结果允许一次带原因记录的重试；"
   575	                "权限拒绝与限流绝不重试；探测间 sleep 限速"
   576	            ),
   577	        },
   578	        "summary": {
   579	            "probed_endpoints": len(records),
   580	            "by_state": by_state,
   581	            "by_permission": by_permission,
   582	        },
   583	        "endpoints": [dict(record) for record in records],
   584	        "excluded": [dict(item) for item in excluded],
   585	        "state_evidence": evidence,
   586	        "notes": [
   587	            "矩阵结果只来自本次真实探测，不引用静态 SOURCE_CAPABILITY_MATRIX，也不按积分等级推测",
   588	            "矩阵不包含真实响应正文、Token、Cookie 或 Authorization；错误文本经脱敏清洗",
   589	            "原始响应以结构化元数据（行/列数、列名、日期范围）与 SHA-256 摘要形式留存",
   590	            *notes,
   591	        ],
   592	    }
   593	
   594	
   595	_CATEGORY_LABELS = {
   596	    CATEGORY_FINANCIAL: "财务",
   597	    CATEGORY_GOVERNANCE: "治理事件",
   598	    CATEGORY_MARKET: "市场行为",
   599	}
   600	
   601	
   602	def _md_escape(value: Any) -> str:
   603	    text = "" if value is None else str(value)
   604	    return text.replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ")
   605	
   606	
   607	def annotate_row_cap_hits(matrix: Mapping[str, Any], *, cap: int = ROW_CAP_DEFAULT) -> None:
   608	    """Flag any record that reached the upstream row cap.
   609	
   610	    Scope-agnostic on purpose (001A-R1): even a symbol-scoped probe can hit
   611	    the cap when upstream ignores filter params, and that must stay visible.
   612	    """
   613	
   614	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   615	        if record.get("row_count") is not None and record["row_count"] >= cap:
   616	            record["row_cap_suspected"] = True
   617	    if any(record.get("row_cap_suspected") for record in matrix["endpoints"]):
   618	        notes = matrix["notes"]
   619	        note = _ROW_CAP_NOTE.format(cap=cap)
   620	        if note not in notes:
   620	        if note not in notes:
   621	            notes.append(note)
   622	
   623	
   624	def render_markdown(matrix: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
   625	    probe = matrix["probe"]
   626	    summary = matrix["summary"]
   627	    lines: list[str] = []
   628	    lines.append(f"# Tushare 2000 积分真实权限矩阵（{matrix['task_id']}）")
   629	    lines.append("")
   630	    lines.append(f"- 生成时间：{matrix['generated_at']}")
   631	    lines.append(f"- Token 状态：`{matrix['token_status']}`（不记录 Token 任何明文信息）")
   632	    token_source = matrix.get("token_source")
   633	    if token_source:
   634	        source_label = (
   635	            "git-ignored .env 文件"
   636	            if token_source == TOKEN_SOURCE_DOTENV
   637	            else "继承环境变量"
   638	        )
   639	        source_line = f"- Token 来源：`{token_source}`（{source_label}）"
   640	        if matrix.get("token_env_override_applied"):
   641	            source_line += "；继承环境变量中的 TUSHARE_TOKEN 已被显式 .env 值覆盖，矩阵归属以 .env 为准"
   642	        lines.append(source_line)
   643	    lines.append(
   644	        f"- 探测标的：`{probe['symbol']}`（{probe['exchange']}），"
   645	        f"探测日 `{probe['probe_date']}`，回退日 `{probe['fallback_date']}`"
   646	    )
   647	    lines.append(f"- 探测策略：{probe['policy']}")
   648	    lines.append("")
   649	    lines.append("## 汇总")
   650	    lines.append("")
   651	    lines.append(f"- 实测 endpoint 数：{summary['probed_endpoints']}")
   652	    for state, count in sorted(summary["by_state"].items()):
   653	        lines.append(f"- `{state}`：{count}")
   654	    for status, count in sorted(summary["by_permission"].items()):
   655	        lines.append(f"- 权限 `{status}`：{count}")
   656	    lines.append("")
   657	    lines.append("## 实测权限矩阵")
   658	    lines.append("")
   659	    lines.append(
   660	        "| endpoint | 类别 | 状态 | 权限 | 行数 | 数据时间范围 | 额外付费 | 重试 | SHA-256(前12位) |"
   661	    )
   662	    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---:|---|---|---|---|")
   663	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   664	        digest = record.get("response_sha256") or ""
   665	        time_range = record.get("time_range")
   666	        time_range_text = (
   667	            f"{time_range['min']}~{time_range['max']}({time_range['field']})"
   668	            if time_range
   669	            else "-"
   670	        )
   671	        retry = "是" if record.get("attempts", 1) > 1 else "否"
   672	        lines.append(
   673	            "| {endpoint} | {category} | `{state}` | {permission} | {rows} | {time_range} "
   674	            "| {extra_paid} | {retry} | `{digest}` |".format(
   675	                endpoint=_md_escape(record["endpoint"]),
   676	                category=_CATEGORY_LABELS.get(record["category"], record["category"]),
   677	                state=_md_escape(record["state"]),
   678	                permission=_md_escape(record["permission_status"]),
   679	                rows=record.get("row_count") if record.get("row_count") is not None else "-",
   680	                time_range=_md_escape(time_range_text),
   681	                extra_paid="是" if record.get("extra_paid") else "否",
   682	                retry=retry,
   683	                digest=digest[:12] if digest else "-",
   684	            )
   685	        )
   686	    lines.append("")
   687	    lines.append("### 探测参数与说明")
   688	    lines.append("")
   689	    lines.append("| endpoint | 查询参数摘要 | scope | 说明 |")
   690	    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
   691	    for record in matrix["endpoints"]:
   692	        params = record.get("params") or {}
   693	        digest = ", ".join(f"{key}={value}" for key, value in sorted(params.items()))
   694	        lines.append(
   695	            f"| {_md_escape(record['endpoint'])} | {_md_escape(digest)} "
   696	            f"| {_md_escape(record.get('scope'))} | {_md_escape(record.get('note'))} |"
   697	        )
   698	    lines.append("")
   699	    lines.append("## 明确排除（未实测，不推测）")
   700	    lines.append("")
   701	    lines.append("| 范围 | 定性 | 是否额外付费 | 原因 |")
   702	    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
   703	    for item in matrix["excluded"]:
   704	        lines.append(
   705	            f"| {_md_escape(item['endpoint'])} | `{item['classification']}` "
   706	            f"| {item['extra_paid']} | {_md_escape(item['reason'])} |"
   707	        )
   708	    lines.append("")
   709	    lines.append("## 状态证据")
   710	    lines.append("")
   711	    lines.append("| 状态 | 真实运行观察到 | 真实示例 | fixture 证据 |")
   712	    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|")
   713	    for state, info in matrix["state_evidence"].items():
   714	        observed = "是" if info["observed_in_real_run"] else "否（未验证）"
   715	        examples = ", ".join(info.get("real_run_examples") or []) or "-"
   716	        fixture = info.get("fixture") or "-"
   717	        lines.append(f"| `{state}` | {observed} | {_md_escape(examples)} | {_md_escape(fixture)} |")
   718	    lines.append("")
   719	    lines.append("## 备注")
   720	    lines.append("")
